# BNG3 release capability inventory

This is the primary release-scope document. Every row is required. Scope is the complete canonical BNG2, NFsim and PyBioNetGen user-facing union plus BNG3 additions and SBML Level 3 Core, Multi and comp. Optional installation does not reduce support.

Audit snapshot: `918a5fb1`. Source pins are in `DECISIONS.md` and `evidence/upstreams.lock.yml`. Source locations below are relative to the named pinned upstream. BNG3 paths refer to the audit snapshot.

**Status semantics:** `done` requires the full named independent check on main; `wrong` identifies a demonstrated contract mismatch; `missing` means implementation or required verification remains to be completed. Thus missing is not a claim that no implementation exists. No new scientific suite ran in this documentation audit. Existing local tests are inspection evidence; they are not relabeled as current release qualification.

`release-capability[ID]` is a proposed case identity to be implemented in the independent release framework, not an existing test. Existing test paths name the starting harness and will be extended with each row's case. Every case will check valid values, defaults, invalid inputs, and applicable interaction combinations. Runtime tests must execute the downstream consumer. The matrix will record exact case selectors and results before a row becomes done.

The accompanying `capability-matrix.yml` is JSON-compatible YAML for `provenance/capability-matrix.yml`. It preserves the old primary row IDs. Inventory promotion also updates its schema validator and consumers; merely replacing a YAML file does not finish that migration.
